Breathing

Backtest (2026-10-07): putting the strike at the inhale top is about the same as selling 4-6% out of the money, by the rule pierced 33%, portfolio -1.75% per year (other tickers), unsupported; 2-week expiry needs inhale top +8%, 3-4 weeks needs +10% or more to push the pierce rate below 15%, and at that point the return is about the same as not selling.

Transition point = the first bar in the inhale whose close is below the high of the bar 2 bars earlier; exhale = pullback or range, until the close crosses back above the inhale top. 'Historically reclaimed' = the share of this ticker's past exhales that reclaimed the inhale top within the same number of days, which is the pierce risk of placing the strike at the inhale top.
